Campaign, seed 644, difficulty Recruit, permadeath off, scheme TwoRollAverage, 10 maps
-- Before map 1 of 10: Starting Alone; the purse holds 500 --
== Roster ==
  Alder Fenn (captain): Cadet L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3; promotes at 3: Marshal, Ranger or Vanguard
  Wren: Cadet L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Sword 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Teodor: Pikeman L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3, 3: Family Lance 40/40
  Ottilie: Bowman L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Bow 40/40, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Pell: Adept L1, EXP 0; 1: Cinder 8/8, 2: Gust 6/6
  Dunstan: Bulwark L1, EXP 0; 1: Iron Lance 40/40
  Brannock: Cadet L1, EXP 0; 1: Hatchet 45/45, 2: Field Dressing 3/3
Deploys to Starting Alone: Alder Fenn (deploy 1 of 7)
== Keep ==
Purse: 500
Rooms: beds: 7/12; a fallen member keeps their bed
  bunk: Bunk room, 400, +2 beds, built 0 of 2
  forge: Forge, 600, Refine +5 acc or +1 power a step, built 0 of 1; opens once the_tollgate is won
  barracks: Barracks, 500, +2 beds and 4 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won
  barracks_wing: Barracks wing, 300, +1 bed and 3 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won; needs the Barracks first
  Stores: common 0, frozen iron 0; a step costs one and 100, shop weapons to +2 on common, the main line's signatures to +3 on frozen iron
Walls and ditches: The keep's menu opens after the raid on it (ironwake_raid, map 6) is fought
== Quests ==
No side map is open.
== Shop ==
Shop: iron_sword 400, field_dressing 150; a seal for promotion costs 500
Trials in place of a seal (one attempt per unit and class before each map): Bulwark, Outrider
March (march): map 1, Starting Alone. Defeat every enemy by the end of turn 10. Captain Fenn must survive.
> march
Map 1 of 10: Starting Alone, seed 644
-- Starting Alone --
Ironwake, before sunrise. Rime whitens the walls to the hoardings. In
the yard Hask has both hands on the girth of Alder's horse.
Hask: He still blows his belly out for the cinch. You were nine the
first time he had you off on the road.
Hask waits for the horse to breathe out and takes the strap up a hole.
Alder Fenn: None of them has reported, sir.
Hask: They won't come to a keep. They'll come to whoever walks to their
door.
Alder unfolds the list against the saddle. Ten names in a clerk's hand.
The first is struck through, and Alder's thumb stays on it.
Alder Fenn: Who struck the first line?
Hask: The clerk copied the armistice roll, and the roll is eight winters
stale. Ask at every door twice.
He hands up a purse. It is lighter than a purse for ten should be, and
he does not pretend otherwise.
Hask: Bread and a bed for ten until the thaw. Not a week past it.
Hask: Aldmere will swear it sent its men, and Sallow will swear Aldmere
lied. Kestrow won't answer the letter.
Hask: Count them before you draw. The bowman will keep to the high
ground and let you come to him.
Alder Fenn: I'll write from each door.
Hask: Don't write, Alder. Bring them home.
The sergeant: She sticks at the half in a frost, Warden. Give her a
minute.
Hask puts two fingers under the girth. It is tight. He checks it again
anyway, and behind him the gate starts to grind open.
(forecast <unit> <target> prints a strike and its counter before you
take it. threat <unit> prints who can reach you next phase. recall
rewinds to a player turn you name; you have three.)

Objective: Defeat every enemy by the end of turn 10. Captain Fenn must survive.

Starting Alone won: rout; reward 300, the purse holds 800; nobody fell
-- After Starting Alone --
Three dead on the waystation road. The keeper comes out of the fort with
a barrow and no questions.
The keeper: Help me get them off the road before dark. I've a milk cart
through at first light.
Alder Fenn: Has anyone come through for Ironwake?
The keeper: A Kestrow chaplain, two days back, walking your way. Men
went after her on the mill road.
Before the barrow takes them, Alder goes through their coats. Each
carries the same coin: silver so new the rims are still sharp.
Alder turns one over, then tips them all into Hask's purse and draws the
string.

-- Before map 2 of 10: The Mill; the purse holds 800 --

Map 2 of 10: The Mill, seed 645
-- The Mill --
The mill road follows a stream that outlived its mill. On the far bank
the miller's house has its shutters barred from inside, and smoke still
comes from the chimney.
The woodpile by its door is down to the last row. Two arrows stand in
the shutter.
Alder finds the line on the list. Maud. Kestrow. Chaplain.
Alder ties the horse to the dead mill's wheel and goes on afoot.
(This map is lost if Maud falls. She strikes at range 1 or 2 with
Radiance and heals with her Salve. A fight within 6 tiles of a sleeping
group wakes it; threat names what a stop would wake.)

Objective: Defeat every enemy by the end of turn 9. Captain Fenn and Maud must survive.

-- After The Mill --
When the last raider is down, Maud comes from the house with a basin
under one arm, in a chaplain's grey gone black at the hem.
Maud: Your fingertips are white. Sit on the step and give me your hands.
Alder sits, because she already has one of them.
Alder Fenn: It's colder where I grew up. They always go like that.
Maud: They always go like that until they go black. The other one,
captain.
Alder Fenn: Maud, of Kestrow. You're on my list.
Maud: They stopped me at the crossing and asked for the rite-keeper by
that name. I hadn't said it to anyone south of Kestrow.
Maud: Did any of them say a name before they fell?
Alder Fenn: No.
Alder takes a stub of pencil from the coat and puts a small mark beside
her name.
Maud dries her hands and goes to the dead. At each of the four she
kneels and says the prayer whole. Where the name goes she stops, waits
as long as a name would take, and goes on.

-- Before map 3 of 10: Saltmarsh Ford; the purse holds 1400 --

  barracks: Barracks, 500, +2 beds and 4 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won
  barracks_wing: Barracks wing, 300, +1 bed and 3 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won; needs the Barracks first

== Keep ==

> quest maud_1 brannock
-- The Lazar House --
The keeper at Sorrel Bend has stopped writing. Maud's order kept the
lazar house there until the armistice, then called its sisters home and
left the sick with him. Maud packs the basin. "The carts come at dusk.
Nobody strips this door till then. You won't be thanked."

(Hold out five turns. The lanes in are announced. A unit that ends its
move on the bar beside a lane, 8,1 north or 10,4 east, closes that lane
while it stays there; what the bar blocks waits behind it and comes
through, one a turn, once the bar is left. The ford south cannot be
barred.)

Side map: The Lazar House, seed 667
Maud goes with Brannock. This map is lost if Maud falls; who falls here comes back wounded.
  when one of yours stops on 8,1: 8,0 becomes wall while one of yours stays on 8,1; it gives way when 8,1 is left.
  when one of yours stops on 10,4: 11,4 becomes wall while one of yours stays on 10,4; it gives way when 10,4 is left.
  turn 2, enemy phase: a brigand arrives at 8,0 (aggressive). A unit or a wall on 8,0 holds it back, and it lands at the first enemy phase that starts with 8,0 open.
  turn 2, enemy phase: a soldier arrives at 11,4 (aggressive). A unit or a wall on 11,4 holds it back, and it lands at the first enemy phase that starts with 11,4 open.
  turn 3, enemy phase: an archer arrives at 6,7 (aggressive). A unit or a wall on 6,7 holds it back, and it lands at the first enemy phase that starts with 6,7 open.
  turn 3, enemy phase: a hexer arrives at 8,0 (aggressive). A unit or a wall on 8,0 holds it back, and it lands at the first enemy phase that starts with 8,0 open.
  turn 4, enemy phase: a brigand arrives at 11,4 (aggressive). A unit or a wall on 11,4 holds it back, and it lands at the first enemy phase that starts with 11,4 open.
  turn 5, enemy phase: a brigand arrives at 6,7 (aggressive). A unit or a wall on 6,7 holds it back, and it lands at the first enemy phase that starts with 6,7 open.
  turn 5, enemy phase: an archer arrives at 11,2 (aggressive). A unit or a wall on 11,2 holds it back, and it lands at the first enemy phase that starts with 11,2 open.
Objective: Hold out until the end of turn 5. Maud must survive.

> quest pell_1 brannock
-- The Burned School --
The shrine-school at Aldmere burned around Pell when she was fourteen.
The roof never went back on, and the rain has had eight years at the
shelves. Now somebody brings torches for what it left. "Whatever's dry
comes out. The catalogue's chained under the lectern, so I'll know what
we leave."

(Get Pell off the east edge, 12,3 to 12,5, by the end of turn 7; anyone
still on the board when she exits is left behind and falls. The map is
lost if she falls. Three chests hold what the fire missed. The burners
come in from the west road from turn 1, announced.)

-- Before map 6 of 10: Raid on Ironwake; the purse holds 3030 --
== Roster ==
  Alder Fenn (captain): Vanguard L5, EXP 28; 1: Iron Sword 19/40 (repair 210), 2: Field Dressing 2/3; ladder: Vanguard, then Champion at 10
  Wren: Cadet L3, EXP 5, Wounded (1); 1: Iron Sword 29/40 (repair 110), 2: Field Dressing 2/3
  Teodor: Pikeman L3, EXP 44; 1: Iron Lance 37/40 (repair 30), 2: Field Dressing 3/3, 3: Family Lance 40/40
  Ottilie: Bowman L3, EXP 10; 1: Iron Bow 38/40 (repair 20), 2: Field Dressing 2/3
  Pell: Adept L3, EXP 0, Wounded (2); 1: Gust 6/6, 2: Cinder 8/8
  Dunstan: Bulwark L3, EXP 10; 1: Iron Lance 38/40 (repair 20)
  Maud: Chaplain L1, EXP 0; 1: Radiance 5/5, 2: Salve 8/8
  Brannock: Cadet L3, EXP 30; 1: Hatchet 41/45 (repair 24), 2: Field Dressing 3/3
  Supports: Alder Fenn and Teodor C
Deploys to Raid on Ironwake: Alder Fenn, Wren, Teodor, Ottilie, Pell, Dunstan (deploy 6 of 8)
The last seat: Keziah (Reaver) or Rook (Skyrider) (pick <unit>). The one passed on rides home.
Keziah: "Everyone here is offering you a promise. Mine's hungry, and you'll hear it ask."
Rook: "She's the last one. Pick me and you're the second person alive who knows her name."
They come back before the keep. Turned, they join only if a bed is free, and a death never frees one.
== Keep ==
Purse: 3030
Rooms: beds: 8/12; a fallen member keeps their bed
  bunk: Bunk room, 400, +2 beds, built 0 of 2
  forge: Forge, 600, Refine +5 acc or +1 power a step, built 1 of 1
  barracks: Barracks, 500, +2 beds and 4 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won
  barracks_wing: Barracks wing, 300, +1 bed and 3 hires at 300, built 0 of 1; opens once ironwake_raid is won; needs the Barracks first
  Stores: common 2, frozen iron 0; a step costs one and 100, shop weapons to +2 on common, the main line's signatures to +3 on frozen iron
Walls and ditches: The keep's menu opens after the raid on it (ironwake_raid, map 6) is fought
== Quests ==
Side maps (the member and one ally you pick, not the captain; a lost side map never ends the campaign):
  teodor_1: Teodor's quest 1, The Old Watch (quest teodor_1 <ally>); permadeath is off: who falls comes back wounded
  maud_1: Maud's quest 1, The Lazar House (quest maud_1 <ally>); permadeath is off: who falls comes back wounded
Side maps won:
